        Idea:
Refactor clip resize handler in TimelineCanvas.tsx to use state machine + AbortController pattern from useClipDrag refactor. Fix 9 known bugs: session leaks, pointer capture races, stale closures, gestureOwner lockout. Apply idle/pending/resizing state machine, AbortController cleanup, no pointer capture, single latestRef.

    "description": "Create `reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useClipResizeGesture.ts` implementing the full state machine hook.\n\n**Types to define inline:**\n- `ResizeMachineState` union: `{ phase: 'idle' } | { phase: 'pending'; controller: AbortController; session: InternalResizeSession } | { phase: 'resizing'; controller: AbortController; session: InternalResizeSession }`\n- `InternalResizeSession` extending `ClipEdgeResizeSession` with `startClientX: number`, `claimedGestureOwner: boolean`\n- `UseClipResizeGestureLatest` ref type with: `gestureOwner`, `setGestureOwner`, `rows`, `shotGroups`, `onActionResizeStart`, `onActionResizing`, `onClipEdgeResizeEnd`, `interactionStateRef`, `setInputModalityFromPointerType`, `timeToPixel`, `pixelToTime`, `pixelsPerSecond`, `minDuration`. **NOT** `positionedShotGroups` \u2014 computed on demand.\n\n**Move these functions from TimelineCanvas.tsx into the hook file:**\n- `createResizePreviewStore` (TC:358-417) \u2014 factory, instantiated inside hook\n- `resolveClipEdgeResizeContext` (TC:663-746) \u2014 kept as pure function taking `(rows, shotGroups, resizePreviewSnapshot, rowId, clipId, edge, dataRef)`. Must compute `positionedShotGroups` inline from raw inputs.\n- `computeResizePreview` (TC:766-800)\n- `getResolvedResizeAction` (TC:748-764)\n- `updateResize` (TC:802-825)\n- `clearResizePreview` (TC:659-661)\n- Helper functions: `getResizePreviewIds` (TC:228-240), `getOverrideMapForUpdates` (TC:211-226), `getPreviewUpdatesFromSnapshot` (TC:242-281), `getOuterReleaseUpdates` (TC:283-324), `clampFreeBoundaryTime` (TC:326-349), `getGroupPreviewKey` (TC:204), `getUpdateForClip` (TC:206-209), `getAudioResizeLimits` (TC:142-187), `collectSiblingTimes` (TC:189-202)\n\n**Hook implementation:**\n- `latestRef` pattern matching useClipDrag.ts:114-145 \u2014 update every render, stable effect deps\n- `useSyncExternalStore` on the preview store, return snapshot\n- Single `useEffect` with deps `[dataRef, timelineWrapperRef]`:\n  - `effectController` AbortController for effect-scoped listeners\n  - `document.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown, { signal: effectController.signal })`\n  - `window.addEventListener('blur', handleBlur, { signal: effectController.signal })` \u2014 calls `endSession({ cancelled: true })`\n  - Effect cleanup: `endSession({ cancelled: true })` then `effectController.abort()`\n\n**handlePointerDown:** detect via `closest('[data-resize-edge]')`, extract clipId/rowId/edge from data attrs, call `resolveClipEdgeResizeContext(latestRef.current.rows, latestRef.current.shotGroups, resizePreviewStore.getSnapshot(), ...)`, create session + AbortController, register per-session `pointermove`/`pointerup`/`pointercancel` on `window` with `{ signal }`.\n\n**handlePointerMove (pending phase):** check pointerId match, apply 4px threshold (`RESIZE_ACTIVATION_THRESHOLD_PX`), transition pending\u2192resizing:\n- Check `latestRef.current.gestureOwner` \u2014 abort if not `'none'` or `'trim'`\n- Claim gestureOwner as `'trim'`, set `session.claimedGestureOwner = true`\n- **CRITICAL GUARD:** call `onActionResizeStart` ONLY when `session.context.kind !== 'outer'` (preserves contract from TC:959-964)\n- Set `interactionStateRef.current.resize = true`\n\n**handlePointerMove (resizing phase):** call preview update. **CRITICAL GUARD:** call `onActionResizing` ONLY when `session.context.kind === 'free'` (preserves contract from TC:809-824).\n\n**handlePointerUp:** check pointerId match, call `endSession({ cancelled: false })`.\n**handlePointerCancel:** call `endSession({ cancelled: true })`.\n\n**endSession({ cancelled }):**\n1. Get current state; if idle, return\n2. Abort controller (cleans all per-session listeners)\n3. If `phase === 'pending'`: clear preview, reset `resizeClampedActionId`\n4. If `phase === 'resizing'`: clear `interactionStateRef.current.resize`, call `notifyInteractionEndIfIdle`, compute final preview, call `onClipEdgeResizeEnd`, clear preview\n5. If `session.claimedGestureOwner`: call `setGestureOwner('none')`\n6. Set state to `{ phase: 'idle' }`\n\n**Return:** `{ resizePreviewSnapshot, resizeClampedActionId, resizeSessionRef }`\n\n**Reference useClipDrag.ts extensively** for patterns: state machine (line 102), latestRef (114-145), AbortController (351-352, 466-468, 475-477), pointerId matching (356, 386-387, 443), blur handler (471-473), endSession cleanup order, effect cleanup (484-487).\n\n**Note on pendingOpsRef:** The plan mentions it as bug #8. Current resize code does NOT use pendingOpsRef \u2014 it's not in the resize callback chain. Skip adding it unless you find evidence it's needed; the plan's own analysis says 'either dead code or a missing feature'.",

(flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

    "description": "Create `reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useClipResizeGesture.ts` implementing the full state machine hook.\n\n**Types to define inline:**\n- `ResizeMachineState` union: `{ phase: 'idle' } | { phase: 'pending'; controller: AbortController; session: InternalResizeSession } | { phase: 'resizing'; controller: AbortController; session: InternalResizeSession }`\n- `InternalResizeSession` extending `ClipEdgeResizeSession` with `startClientX: number`, `claimedGestureOwner: boolean`\n- `UseClipResizeGestureLatest` ref type with: `gestureOwner`, `setGestureOwner`, `rows`, `shotGroups`, `onActionResizeStart`, `onActionResizing`, `onClipEdgeResizeEnd`, `interactionStateRef`, `setInputModalityFromPointerType`, `timeToPixel`, `pixelToTime`, `pixelsPerSecond`, `minDuration`. **NOT** `positionedShotGroups` \u2014 computed on demand.\n\n**Move these functions from TimelineCanvas.tsx into the hook file:**\n- `createResizePreviewStore` (TC:358-417) \u2014 factory, instantiated inside hook\n- `resolveClipEdgeResizeContext` (TC:663-746) \u2014 kept as pure function taking `(rows, shotGroups, resizePreviewSnapshot, rowId, clipId, edge, dataRef)`. Must compute `positionedShotGroups` inline from raw inputs.\n- `computeResizePreview` (TC:766-800)\n- `getResolvedResizeAction` (TC:748-764)\n- `updateResize` (TC:802-825)\n- `clearResizePreview` (TC:659-661)\n- Helper functions: `getResizePreviewIds` (TC:228-240), `getOverrideMapForUpdates` (TC:211-226), `getPreviewUpdatesFromSnapshot` (TC:242-281), `getOuterReleaseUpdates` (TC:283-324), `clampFreeBoundaryTime` (TC:326-349), `getGroupPreviewKey` (TC:204), `getUpdateForClip` (TC:206-209), `getAudioResizeLimits` (TC:142-187), `collectSiblingTimes` (TC:189-202)\n\n**Hook implementation:**\n- `latestRef` pattern matching useClipDrag.ts:114-145 \u2014 update every render, stable effect deps\n- `useSyncExternalStore` on the preview store, return snapshot\n- Single `useEffect` with deps `[dataRef, timelineWrapperRef]`:\n  - `effectController` AbortController for effect-scoped listeners\n  - `document.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown, { signal: effectController.signal })`\n  - `window.addEventListener('blur', handleBlur, { signal: effectController.signal })` \u2014 calls `endSession({ cancelled: true })`\n  - Effect cleanup: `endSession({ cancelled: true })` then `effectController.abort()`\n\n**handlePointerDown:** detect via `closest('[data-resize-edge]')`, extract clipId/rowId/edge from data attrs, call `resolveClipEdgeResizeContext(latestRef.current.rows, latestRef.current.shotGroups, resizePreviewStore.getSnapshot(), ...)`, create session + AbortController, register per-session `pointermove`/`pointerup`/`pointercancel` on `window` with `{ signal }`.\n\n**handlePointerMove (pending phase):** check pointerId match, apply 4px threshold (`RESIZE_ACTIVATION_THRESHOLD_PX`), transition pending\u2192resizing:\n- Check `latestRef.current.gestureOwner` \u2014 abort if not `'none'` or `'trim'`\n- Claim gestureOwner as `'trim'`, set `session.claimedGestureOwner = true`\n- **CRITICAL GUARD:** call `onActionResizeStart` ONLY when `session.context.kind !== 'outer'` (preserves contract from TC:959-964)\n- Set `interactionStateRef.current.resize = true`\n\n**handlePointerMove (resizing phase):** call preview update. **CRITICAL GUARD:** call `onActionResizing` ONLY when `session.context.kind === 'free'` (preserves contract from TC:809-824).\n\n**handlePointerUp:** check pointerId match, call `endSession({ cancelled: false })`.\n**handlePointerCancel:** call `endSession({ cancelled: true })`.\n\n**endSession({ cancelled }):**\n1. Get current state; if idle, return\n2. Abort controller (cleans all per-session listeners)\n3. If `phase === 'pending'`: clear preview, reset `resizeClampedActionId`\n4. If `phase === 'resizing'`: clear `interactionStateRef.current.resize`, call `notifyInteractionEndIfIdle`, compute final preview, call `onClipEdgeResizeEnd`, clear preview\n5. If `session.claimedGestureOwner`: call `setGestureOwner('none')`\n6. Set state to `{ phase: 'idle' }`\n\n**Return:** `{ resizePreviewSnapshot, resizeClampedActionId, resizeSessionRef }`\n\n**Reference useClipDrag.ts extensively** for patterns: state machine (line 102), latestRef (114-145), AbortController (351-352, 466-468, 475-477), pointerId matching (356, 386-387, 443), blur handler (471-473), endSession cleanup order, effect cleanup (484-487).\n\n**Note on pendingOpsRef:** The plan mentions it as bug #8. Current resize code does NOT use pendingOpsRef \u2014 it's not in the resize callback chain. Skip adding it unless you find evidence it's needed; the plan's own analysis says 'either dead code or a missing feature'.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

    "description": "Create `reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useClipResizeGesture.ts` implementing the full state machine hook.\n\n**Types to define inline:**\n- `ResizeMachineState` union: `{ phase: 'idle' } | { phase: 'pending'; controller: AbortController; session: InternalResizeSession } | { phase: 'resizing'; controller: AbortController; session: InternalResizeSession }`\n- `InternalResizeSession` extending `ClipEdgeResizeSession` with `startClientX: number`, `claimedGestureOwner: boolean`\n- `UseClipResizeGestureLatest` ref type with: `gestureOwner`, `setGestureOwner`, `rows`, `shotGroups`, `onActionResizeStart`, `onActionResizing`, `onClipEdgeResizeEnd`, `interactionStateRef`, `setInputModalityFromPointerType`, `timeToPixel`, `pixelToTime`, `pixelsPerSecond`, `minDuration`. **NOT** `positionedShotGroups` \u2014 computed on demand.\n\n**Move these functions from TimelineCanvas.tsx into the hook file:**\n- `createResizePreviewStore` (TC:358-417) \u2014 factory, instantiated inside hook\n- `resolveClipEdgeResizeContext` (TC:663-746) \u2014 kept as pure function taking `(rows, shotGroups, resizePreviewSnapshot, rowId, clipId, edge, dataRef)`. Must compute `positionedShotGroups` inline from raw inputs.\n- `computeResizePreview` (TC:766-800)\n- `getResolvedResizeAction` (TC:748-764)\n- `updateResize` (TC:802-825)\n- `clearResizePreview` (TC:659-661)\n- Helper functions: `getResizePreviewIds` (TC:228-240), `getOverrideMapForUpdates` (TC:211-226), `getPreviewUpdatesFromSnapshot` (TC:242-281), `getOuterReleaseUpdates` (TC:283-324), `clampFreeBoundaryTime` (TC:326-349), `getGroupPreviewKey` (TC:204), `getUpdateForClip` (TC:206-209), `getAudioResizeLimits` (TC:142-187), `collectSiblingTimes` (TC:189-202)\n\n**Hook implementation:**\n- `latestRef` pattern matching useClipDrag.ts:114-145 \u2014 update every render, stable effect deps\n- `useSyncExternalStore` on the preview store, return snapshot\n- Single `useEffect` with deps `[dataRef, timelineWrapperRef]`:\n  - `effectController` AbortController for effect-scoped listeners\n  - `document.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown, { signal: effectController.signal })`\n  - `window.addEventListener('blur', handleBlur, { signal: effectController.signal })` \u2014 calls `endSession({ cancelled: true })`\n  - Effect cleanup: `endSession({ cancelled: true })` then `effectController.abort()`\n\n**handlePointerDown:** detect via `closest('[data-resize-edge]')`, extract clipId/rowId/edge from data attrs, call `resolveClipEdgeResizeContext(latestRef.current.rows, latestRef.current.shotGroups, resizePreviewStore.getSnapshot(), ...)`, create session + AbortController, register per-session `pointermove`/`pointerup`/`pointercancel` on `window` with `{ signal }`.\n\n**handlePointerMove (pending phase):** check pointerId match, apply 4px threshold (`RESIZE_ACTIVATION_THRESHOLD_PX`), transition pending\u2192resizing:\n- Check `latestRef.current.gestureOwner` \u2014 abort if not `'none'` or `'trim'`\n- Claim gestureOwner as `'trim'`, set `session.claimedGestureOwner = true`\n- **CRITICAL GUARD:** call `onActionResizeStart` ONLY when `session.context.kind !== 'outer'` (preserves contract from TC:959-964)\n- Set `interactionStateRef.current.resize = true`\n\n**handlePointerMove (resizing phase):** call preview update. **CRITICAL GUARD:** call `onActionResizing` ONLY when `session.context.kind === 'free'` (preserves contract from TC:809-824).\n\n**handlePointerUp:** check pointerId match, call `endSession({ cancelled: false })`.\n**handlePointerCancel:** call `endSession({ cancelled: true })`.\n\n**endSession({ cancelled }):**\n1. Get current state; if idle, return\n2. Abort controller (cleans all per-session listeners)\n3. If `phase === 'pending'`: clear preview, reset `resizeClampedActionId`\n4. If `phase === 'resizing'`: clear `interactionStateRef.current.resize`, call `notifyInteractionEndIfIdle`, compute final preview, call `onClipEdgeResizeEnd`, clear preview\n5. If `session.claimedGestureOwner`: call `setGestureOwner('none')`\n6. Set state to `{ phase: 'idle' }`\n\n**Return:** `{ resizePreviewSnapshot, resizeClampedActionId, resizeSessionRef }`\n\n**Reference useClipDrag.ts extensively** for patterns: state machine (line 102), latestRef (114-145), AbortController (351-352, 466-468, 475-477), pointerId matching (356, 386-387, 443), blur handler (471-473), endSession cleanup order, effect cleanup (484-487).\n\n**Note on pendingOpsRef:** The plan mentions it as bug #8. Current resize code does NOT use pendingOpsRef \u2014 it's not in the resize callback chain. Skip adding it unless you find evidence it's needed; the plan's own analysis says 'either dead code or a missing feature'.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

Must compute `positionedShotGroups` inline from raw inputs.\n- `computeResizePreview` (TC:766-800)\n- `getResolvedResizeAction` (TC:748-764)\n- `updateResize` (TC:802-825)\n- `clearResizePreview` (TC:659-661)\n- Helper functions: `getResizePreviewIds` (TC:228-240), `getOverrideMapForUpdates` (TC:211-226), `getPreviewUpdatesFromSnapshot` (TC:242-281), `getOuterReleaseUpdates` (TC:283-324), `clampFreeBoundaryTime` (TC:326-349), `getGroupPreviewKey` (TC:204), `getUpdateForClip` (TC:206-209), `getAudioResizeLimits` (TC:142-187), `collectSiblingTimes` (TC:189-202)\n\n**Hook implementation:**\n- `latestRef` pattern matching useClipDrag.ts:114-145 \u2014 update every render, stable effect deps\n- `useSyncExternalStore` on the preview store, return snapshot\n- Single `useEffect` with deps `[dataRef, timelineWrapperRef]`:\n  - `effectController` AbortController for effect-scoped listeners\n  - `document.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown, { signal: effectController.signal })`\n  - `window.addEventListener('blur', handleBlur, { signal: effectController.signal })` \u2014 calls `endSession({ cancelled: true })`\n  - Effect cleanup: `endSession({ cancelled: true })` then `effectController.abort()`\n\n**handlePointerDown:** detect via `closest('[data-resize-edge]')`, extract clipId/rowId/edge from data attrs, call `resolveClipEdgeResizeContext(latestRef.current.rows, latestRef.current.shotGroups, resizePreviewStore.getSnapshot(), ...)`, create session + AbortController, register per-session `pointermove`/`pointerup`/`pointercancel` on `window` with `{ signal }`.\n\n**handlePointerMove (pending phase):** check pointerId match, apply 4px threshold (`RESIZE_ACTIVATION_THRESHOLD_PX`), transition pending\u2192resizing:\n- Check `latestRef.current.gestureOwner` \u2014 abort if not `'none'` or `'trim'`\n- Claim gestureOwner as `'trim'`, set `session.claimedGestureOwner = true`\n- **CRITICAL GUARD:** call `onActionResizeStart` ONLY when `session.context.kind !== 'outer'` (preserves contract from TC:959-964)\n- Set `interactionStateRef.current.resize = true`\n\n**handlePointerMove (resizing phase):** call preview update. **CRITICAL GUARD:** call `onActionResizing` ONLY when `session.context.kind === 'free'` (preserves contract from TC:809-824).\n\n**handlePointerUp:** check pointerId match, call `endSession({ cancelled: false })`.\n**handlePointerCancel:** call `endSession({ cancelled: true })`.\n\n**endSession({ cancelled }):**\n1. Get current state; if idle, return\n2. Abort controller (cleans all per-session listeners)\n3. If `phase === 'pending'`: clear preview, reset `resizeClampedActionId`\n4. If `phase === 'resizing'`: clear `interactionStateRef.current.resize`, call `notifyInteractionEndIfIdle`, compute final preview, call `onClipEdgeResizeEnd`, clear preview\n5. If `session.claimedGestureOwner`: call `setGestureOwner('none')`\n6. Set state to `{ phase: 'idle' }`\n\n**Return:** `{ resizePreviewSnapshot, resizeClampedActionId, resizeSessionRef }`\n\n**Reference useClipDrag.ts extensively** for patterns: state machine (line 102), latestRef (114-145), AbortController (351-352, 466-468, 475-477), pointerId matching (356, 386-387, 443), blur handler (471-473), endSession cleanup order, effect cleanup (484-487).\n\n**Note on pendingOpsRef:** The plan mentions it as bug #8.   "meta_commentary": "**Execution guidance:**\n\nThis is a mechanical refactor with well-defined source and target patterns. The useClipDrag hook is the template \u2014 follow its structure line-by-line for the state machine, latestRef, AbortController, and listener patterns.\n\n**Biggest risk:** Getting the callback guards wrong. The two guards (onActionResizeStart skipped for outer, onActionResizing skipped for non-free) are the most subtle behavioral contracts. Read the existing code at TC:959-964 and TC:809-824 carefully before implementing.\n\n**T1 is the bulk of the work** (~70% of effort). T2 is small and independent \u2014 can be done in parallel with T1. T3 is the integration step where things break. T4 and T5 are verification.\n\n**On helper function placement:** The plan says to move helpers into the hook file or a co-located helpers file. Given the count (~12 helper functions), consider putting the pure utility functions (getResizePreviewIds, getOverrideMapForUpdates, getPreviewUpdatesFromSnapshot, getOuterReleaseUpdates, clampFreeBoundaryTime, getGroupPreviewKey, getUpdateForClip, getAudioResizeLimits, collectSiblingTimes) in a separate `useClipResizeGesture.helpers.ts` file to keep the main hook file under 350 lines. This mirrors useClipDrag having useClipDrag.helpers.ts.\n\n**On the ResizeOverride type:** This is likely defined near createResizePreviewStore. Make sure it's exported from the hook file since TrackListRenderer still needs it for rendering props.\n\n**Test command reminder:** Always `cd reigh-app && npx vitest run --config config/testing/vitest.config.ts` \u2014 NOT bare `npx vitest run` from workspace root.",

- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

'@/tools/video-editor/types/timeline-canvas';\n+\n+const useTimelineEditorDataMock = vi.fn();\n+\n+vi.mock('@/tools/video-editor/contexts/TimelineEditorContext', async () => {\n+  const actual = await useTimelineEditorData: () => useTimelineEditorDataMock(),\n+  };\n+});\n+\n+describe('clip resize regression', () => {\n+  it('resizes from a data-resize-edge handle through the document listeners and releases gesture ownership on pointerup', () => {\n+    const track: TrackDefinition = { id: 'V1', kind: 'visual', label: 'V1' };\n+    const action: TimelineAction = { id: 'clip-1', start: 0, end: 2, effectId: 'effect-clip-1' };\n+    const row: TimelineRow = { id: 'V1', actions: [action] };\n+    const onClipEdgeResizeEnd = vi.fn();\n+    const setGestureOwner = vi.fn();\n+    useTimelineEditorDataMock.mockReturnValue({ dataRef: { current: null } });\n+\n+    const { container } = render(\n+      <TimelineCanvas\n+        rows={[row]}\n+        tracks={[track]}\n+        deviceClass=\"desktop\"\n+        inputModality=\"mouse\"\n+        interactionMode=\"select\"\n+        gestureOwner=\"none\"\n+        scale={1}\n+        scaleWidth={100}\n+        scaleSplitCount={1}\n+        startLeft={0}\n+        rowHeight={48}\n+        minScaleCount={1}\n+        maxScaleCount={10}\n+        selectedTrackId={null}\n+        getActionRender={() => <div>clip</div>}\n+        onSelectTrack={vi.fn()}\n+        onTrackChange={vi.fn()}\n+        onRemoveTrack={vi.fn()}\n+        onTrackDragEnd={vi.fn()}\n+        trackSensors={[] as never}\n+        onCursorDrag={vi.fn()}\n+        onClickTimeArea={vi.fn()}\n+        setInputModalityFromPointerType={vi.fn(() => 'mouse')}\n+        setGestureOwner={setGestureOwner}\n+        onClipEdgeResizeEnd={onClipEdgeResizeEnd}\n+        dragSessionRef={{ current: null }}\n+      />,\n+    );\n+\n+    const actionElement = container.querySelector('[data-action-id=\"clip-1\"]');\n+    const rightHandle = actionElement?.querySelector('[data-resize-edge=\"right\"]');\n+    expect(actionElement).toBeInstanceOf(HTMLElement);\n+    expect(rightHandle).toBeInstanceOf(HTMLElement);\n+    if (!(actionElement instanceof HTMLElement) || !(rightHandle instanceof HTMLElement)) {\n+      throw new Error('expected clip action and right resize handle');\n+    }\n+\n+    fireEvent.pointerDown(rightHandle, {\n+      button: 0,\n+      pointerId: 41,\n+      clientX: 200,\n+    });\n+    fireEvent.pointerMove(rightHandle, {\n+      pointerId: 41,\n+      clientX: 300,\n+    });\n+\n+    expect(actionElement.style.width).toBe('300px');\n+\n+    fireEvent.pointerUp(rightHandle, {\n+      pointerId: 41,\n+      clientX: 300,\n+    });\n+\n+    expect(setGestureOwner).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(1, 'trim');\n+    expect(setGestureOwner).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith('none');\n+    expect(onClipEdgeResizeEnd).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({\n+      cancelled: false,\n+      updates: expect.arrayContaining([\n+        expect.objectContaining({ clipId: 'clip-1', start: 0, end: 3 }),\n+      ]),\n+    }));\n+  });\n+});\n*** End Patch\n"
